# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
Phase 1: Plan (Dev)
Before a single line of code is written, the team must
 define the value they are creating.

Goal: Define requirements, create roadmaps, and organize tasks.
Key Activities: Backlog management, sprint planning, user story creation.
Tools: Jira, Trello, Azure Boards ^K3REXGJM


 ^HZpK7a7i


 ^2Du8EhUS

Phase 2: Code (Dev) ^ZTmV8b5s

Developers write the code and commit it to a shared repository. ^oEg3N7tv

Goal: Write high-quality, version-controlled code. ^S3AJ7SBK

Key Activities: Coding, code reviews, merging branches. ^Mquibags

Tools: Git, GitHub, GitLab, Bitbucket. ^vnS7EaGI

Phase 3: Build (Dev) ^RtZhQBa3

Once code is committed, it is pulled and compiled into an executable format. ^3yfjJ0x2

Goal: Detect compilation errors early and package the application. ^KYCWMMqf

Key Activities: Compiling code, dependency management, creating container images (Docker). ^wvIAE18h

Tools: Maven, Gradle, Docker, Packer. ^hFMu41QZ

Phase 4: Test (Dev) ^e5jLP9kh

The build is tested for bugs, security vulnerabilities, and performance issues. ^mvG6tSo9

Goal: Ensure software quality and prevent bugs from reaching production. ^7Qebwlhz

Key Activities: Unit testing, integration testing, static code analysis. ^wI3kJeHp

Tools: Selenium, JUnit, SonarQube, TestNG. ^2WjlUhHq

Phase 5: Release (Ops) ^glQuluse

The code has passed testing and is ready to be deployed. This phase manages the versioning and approval. ^G13QdlnI

Goal: Schedule and manage the release to specific environments (Staging/Prod). ^xF2VRcNn

Key Activities: Artifact versioning, change management approvals. ^NfwAYmbB

Tools: Jenkins, GitLab CI, CircleCI. ^rLhL9Z63

Phase 6: Deploy (Ops) ^QKLbQnxf

The application is pushed to the production servers. ^EescxaOB

Goal: Deliver the software to end-users with zero downtime. ^7DcM3pRR

Key Activities: Infrastructure provisioning (IaC), configuration management, blue/green deployments. ^x8CzAmER

Tools: Terraform, Ansible, Kubernetes (ArgoCD), AWS CodeDeploy. ^3gZqBetN

Phase 7: Operate (Ops) ^nNYwVjLp

The software is live. This phase involves the day-to-day management of the infrastructure running the app. ^j8bwrzK5

Goal: Ensure system uptime and reliability. ^R6Dof3Km

Key Activities: Server patching, auto-scaling, database management. ^HyW6hBU2

Tools: Chef, Puppet, PowerShell, Ansible. ^okiBxUxa

Phase 8: Monitor (Ops) ^kZbzvmIW

Data is collected from the live application to understand user behavior and system performance. This feedback loops back into the Plan phase. ^Q19O2F1D

Goal: Detect issues immediately and gather insights for future improvements. ^pxPjFekr

Key Activities: Log analysis, performance tracking, alerting. ^MAcEBlfB

Tools: Prometheus, Grafana, Nagios, ELK Stack (Elasticsearch, Logstash, Kibana). ^bOjvCAYx
